Specification, part 7: Migration, tests and delivery

The import from Grafana, the tests and the delivery.

On this page
  1. 15. Import and migration from Grafana
  2. 16. Tests
  3. 17. Delivery

This page comes from docs/07-migration-tests-delivery.md in the repository.

Part 7 of the Hune specification (see 01-overview.md).

This part has three sections. Section 15 gives the import from Grafana and a runbook for the move from Grafana to Hune. Section 16 gives the tests. Section 17 gives the build, the release and the deployment files.

15. Import and migration from Grafana

15.1 Scope and principles

The subcommand hune import-grafana converts the dashboards of Grafana to documents of section 4. It is the only part of Hune that knows Grafana. The crate hune-grafana holds the conversion as a pure function: Grafana JSON and options in, documents and a report out. The binary hune reads the files, writes the report and writes the documents.

The importer obeys these rules:

  • It reads exported files only. It never connects to Grafana, never reads grafana.db and never connects to ClickHouse.
  • It makes a dry run by default. It writes documents only with --write.
  • With --write, it writes through the Store trait of section 5. It is the same code for the volume and for PostgreSQL.
  • Each document that it makes passes the validation of section 4 and the statement check of section 7. These are the same functions that the server uses.
  • It gives each Grafana member exactly one class (15.5). No member is lost without an item in the report.
  • The same input files and options give the same documents, byte for byte (15.13).

15.2 The command

text
hune import-grafana --in <dir>
                     --source-map <grafana-key>=<source-id> [--source-map ...]
                     [--folders <file>]
                     [--link-prefix <old>=<new>]
                     [--accept <file>]
                     [--report <path>]
                     [--nginx-map <file>]
                     [-c <config>] [--write [--replace]]
OptionValueRule
--indirectoryNecessary. The export directory of 15.4. The importer reads only the names of 15.4.
--source-map<grafana-key>=<source-id>Necessary, 1 to 64 times. Maps a Grafana data source to a clickhouse "<id>", postgres "<id>" or mongodb "<id>" block (15.9). The key is a data source uid or name, the name of a data source variable, or the name of an item of __inputs.
--foldersJSON fileOptional. The folder map of 15.7.
--link-prefix<old>=<new>Optional, at most one time. Both parts start and end with /. Example: /grafana/=/dashboards/.
--accepttext fileOptional. The list of accepted losses (15.10).
--reportpath without extensionOptional. Writes <path>.json and <path>.md (15.11).
--nginx-mapfileOptional. Writes the nginx map of 15.17. Needs --link-prefix.
-c, --configconfiguration fileThe option of 13.1. Necessary with --write (13.18). Optional in a dry run: then the importer opens the store read-only and compares (15.13).
--writenoneWrites the documents through the store.
--replacenoneOnly with --write. Replaces a document whose uid exists with other bytes.

An option that is not in this table stops the command with exit code 2. An option given two times stops it with exit code 2, except --source-map. Two --source-map options with the same key stop it with exit code 2.

The importer writes a short summary to the standard output. The summary holds the totals of 15.11 and the reason of the exit code. Errors go to the standard error.

The importer writes --report and --nginx-map files with a temporary file and a rename. It replaces a file that exists. The parent directory must exist. The importer refuses an output path inside a git checkout, with exit code 2. A git checkout is a directory that holds a .git entry, and each directory below it. Reason: a report holds confidential SQL (15.18).

15.3 The export from Grafana

The export is a written procedure that an operator runs on the host of the Grafana instance. Each request is a read-only GET from inside the Grafana container, which has curl. In this example, Grafana serves under /grafana/ (root_url and serve_from_sub_path). Change the path when your instance uses another one.

The procedure needs two inputs. The operator reads them from a copy of grafana.db (the M0 measure of section 18 does the same):

  • stored-forms.txt: one line for each dashboard, <uid> v2 or <uid> v1. The stored form is the member apiVersion in the table resource of the unified storage in grafana.db.
  • The value of V2: the v2 API version that Grafana serves, v2beta1 or v2alpha1.
sh
# Run on the host of the Grafana instance.
# Each request is a read-only GET to Grafana, from inside its own container.
set -eu
umask 077
V2="v2beta1"                                  # the v2 API version of your Grafana
GRAFANA_CONTAINER="grafana"                   # the name of the Grafana container
out="$HOME/grafana-export-$(date -u +%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ)"
mkdir -p "$out/dashboards"
g() { docker exec "$GRAFANA_CONTAINER" curl -sS --fail --max-time 60 "http://localhost:3000/grafana$1"; }
g "/api/health"                                         > "$out/health.json"
g "/api/plugins/grafana-clickhouse-datasource/settings" > "$out/plugin-clickhouse.json"
g "/api/folders?limit=1000"                             > "$out/folders.json"
g "/api/search?type=dash-db&limit=5000"                 > "$out/search.json"
g "/api/dashboards/public-dashboards"                   > "$out/public-dashboards.json"
while read -r uid form; do
  case "$form" in
    v2) g "/apis/dashboard.grafana.app/$V2/namespaces/default/dashboards/$uid" > "$out/dashboards/$uid.json" ;;
    v1) g "/api/dashboards/uid/$uid"                                          > "$out/dashboards/$uid.json" ;;
    *)  echo "bad form for $uid: $form" >&2; exit 2 ;;
  esac
done < stored-forms.txt
(cd "$out" && sha256sum ./*.json dashboards/*.json > SHA256SUMS)

The short links are not in an API answer. The operator writes their list from the copy of grafana.db, on a trusted machine. Then the operator adds the file to the export directory:

sh
sqlite3 -readonly -json grafana.db "SELECT uid, path FROM short_url ORDER BY uid" > "$out/short-urls.json"

The operator copies the directory to a trusted machine. The directory and its files stay outside every git checkout (15.18). The namespace default is the namespace of the Grafana organisation 1. Another organisation has another namespace.

15.4 The input directory

NameNecessaryContent
dashboards/<uid>.jsonyes, 1 to 1000 filesOne dashboard: a v2 resource, a v1 dashboard, or a v1 {dashboard, meta} object.
folders.jsonyesThe answer of GET /api/folders.
search.jsonyes when a raw v1 file existsThe answer of GET /api/search. It gives the folder of a raw v1 dashboard.
health.jsonyesThe answer of GET /api/health. The report records the Grafana version.
plugin-clickhouse.jsonyesThe plugin settings. The report records the plugin version.
public-dashboards.jsonnoThe public dashboards. The report lists them as not imported.
short-urls.jsonnoThe short links. The report lists them as not imported, and 15.17 redirects them.
SHA256SUMSnoWhen it exists, the importer checks each listed file before it reads it.

These rules apply to the input:

  • Each file is UTF-8 JSON of 32 MiB at most. A duplicate member, a byte order mark or data after the value stops the command with exit code 2.
  • A symbolic link in the directory stops the command with exit code 2.
  • The importer ignores other names and lists them in the report (input.ignored_files).
  • A file listed in SHA256SUMS that differs from its digest stops the command with exit code 2.

The importer finds the form of each dashboard file from its members:

MembersFormUidFolder uid
apiVersion = dashboard.grafana.app/v2alpha1 or dashboard.grafana.app/v2beta1v2metadata.namemetadata.annotations["grafana.app/folder"]
dashboard and meta (objects)v1 wrappeddashboard.uidmeta.folderUid
schemaVersion (number) and panels (array)v1 rawuidfolderUid of the uid in search.json

Another apiVersion, or a file that fits no row, stops the command with exit code 2. The file stem must equal the uid of the file, else exit code 2. An empty or absent folder uid means the root of Grafana.

15.5 The four classes

ClassMeaningListed in the reportNeeds acceptance
mappedThe document gives the same behaviour.No, counted only.No
changedThe document gives the behaviour in another form or with a known difference.YesYes
droppedThe member is not in the document, by a rule of this specification.Yes, except metadataYes, except metadata
unsupportedHune cannot give the behaviour, or the member is not in the measured set.YesYes

A metadata member is a dropped member that has no behaviour in Hune. The tables of 15.6 mark them "metadata". The report counts them for each dashboard, and they need no acceptance.

Each unsupported row of 15.6 names a fallback. The importer applies the fallback only when the item is accepted (15.10). When an unsupported item is not accepted, the dashboard is blocked and the importer writes no document (exit code 1).

The importer walks every member of each input file. A member that no row of 15.6 names is unsupported with the reason "unknown member" and the fallback "member removed". So a Grafana feature that this specification does not know is never lost without an item.

One exception applies: an unknown member whose value is null, false, 0, "", [] or {} is metadata. Such a value turns no feature on. Example: "fillScreen": false of a v2 row is metadata, and "fillScreen": true is unsupported.

The exception does not apply to a false or a 0 that turns off a feature that Grafana shows by default. Such a value changes the view, so it is never metadata. The rows of 15.6 name each case. They are custom.axisGridShow = false and wideLayout = false of a stat. A barchart maps custom.axisGridShow, custom.fillOpacity and custom.lineWidth (4.18, 4.30), so false and 0 keep their effect there. The list comes from a probe of Grafana.

15.6 The mapping table

The column "Hune" names the member of the document format of section 4. The column "v2" gives the path below the v2 resource. The column "v1" gives the path in a v1 dashboard. In the v2 form, E is spec.elements.<key>.spec (one panel), and Q is one query of the panel (E.data.spec.queries[i].spec).

In the v2 form, the members of the plugin are in Q.query.spec: rawSql, format, editorType, queryType, builderOptions and meta. In the v1 form, they are in the target. The query table below names them without a prefix.

A mapped value can break a range of section 4: a length, a count or a number. Then the item is unsupported. The fallback of its row applies; a row without a fallback gives "the dashboard is not imported".

Dashboard members:

v2v1ClassHuneReason or fallback
metadata.nameuidmappeduid and its location (4.5)A uid outside the rule of 4.3 is changed to a new uid (15.8).

Another text (GMT, EST, a name with a blank) is unsupported; fallback: browser.

Another duration is changed to the nearest larger choice (example: 5s gives 10s, 2m gives 5m); a duration above 1d is changed to 1d. A text that is not a duration is unsupported; fallback: null.

An empty title is changed to Link. A URL that breaks the prefix rule of 4.23 is unsupported; fallback: item removed. The URL follows the rewrite of --link-prefix, as a panel link does.

spec.annotations[] with a ClickHouse or a PostgreSQL data source of the source map and a queryannotations.list item, target.rawSqlmappedone item of annotations (4.5b)name, the source (the source map, as for a panel query), sql (target.rawSql as lines, checked with the dialect of the kind: 7.18 or 7.26), color (iconColor), enabled (enable) and panels (filter). More than 8 items: each item after the 8th is unsupported; fallback: item removed. A name that repeats is changed to a new name (<name> 2).
item, iconColoriconColormappedcolorA token or a colour of 4.19 maps. An absent, empty or other value is changed to #808080, the colour that Grafana draws for a missing value.
item, enableenablemappedenabledfalse gives enabled = false.
item, filter with exclude = false and idsfiltermappedpanelsThe ids of the panels of the type timeseries that exist in the imported document map. An id of a panel of another type or of a panel that does not exist is changed: it leaves the list. An empty list is unsupported; fallback: item removed. A list of more than 200 ids is unsupported too; the importer does not read it.
item, filter with exclude = truefilterchangedpanelsThe importer lists each timeseries panel that the filter does not exclude. A panel that a person adds later is not in the list. An empty list is unsupported; fallback: item removed.
item, an absent filternonemappedpanels absentThe item draws on each time series.
item, hidehidedroppednoneMetadata when true. When false, it is listed: the page has no toggle for an annotation.
item, mappings with a membermappingsunsupportednoneHune reads the columns by their names only (7.37). Fallback: item removed. The report names the mapped fields.
spec.annotations[] with a MongoDB data source of the source map and a queryannotations.list item, target.queryText, target.collection, target.databasemappedone item of annotations (4.5b)The members of the row above. sql is the object of 7.29 that the importer builds from queryText, collection and database, as for a panel query of this plugin (its rows of the query members). The checks of 7.36 run with the use annotation. A fault of the query is unsupported; fallback: item removed.
item of a MongoDB data source, target.queryType other than tabletarget.queryTypechangednoneHune runs the query of an annotation with the format table (7.37).
item of a PostgreSQL data source, target.editorMode = builder with a rawSqltarget.editorModeunsupportednoneHune has no query builder for PostgreSQL. Fallback: the rawSql stays as the SQL text, and the builder members are removed. The members of a code query (editorMode = code, rawQuery, sql, table) are metadata.

item, a data source of another type, or a data source that is not in the map (Prometheus, Loki, "-- Grafana --" with tags, and the like)datasourceunsupportednoneAn annotation runs on a ClickHouse, a PostgreSQL or a MongoDB source of the map. Fallback: item removed.
item, no target.rawSql (a SQL data source), or no target.queryText or target.collection (a MongoDB data source)targetunsupportednoneThe item has no query. Fallback: item removed.
item, target.format other than tabletarget.formatchangednoneHune runs the query of an annotation with the format table (7.37).
item, another member (type, showIn, limit, tags, expr, target.* that Hune does not read, a member of the plugin of another kind)the sameunsupportednoneFallback: member removed. The default rule of 15.5 applies, and a value false, 0, "", [] or {} is metadata.

The columns of the result are the columns of 7.37. A query for Grafana that selects timeEnd works as it is in Hune. A query that selects time_end or end gets no region in both products. A query that has no text column shows the first text column in Grafana, and it shows no text in Hune.

A query that returns HTML in the text gives the error bad_chars in Hune. The import runs no query (19.20e). So the report adds one changed item at the SQL of each mapped item. Its text is "the SQL of the annotation <name> is not checked against the rules of 7.37".

The v2 form has the same items in spec.annotations[].spec. The query is in .spec.query.spec, and the members name, enable, hide, iconColor, filter and mappings have the names of the v1 form. The form v1 was read on exports of Grafana: the built-in item and an item with no query. It was also read on the measure of 19.20e. No export that the project has seen holds a v2 annotation with a query. So the paths of the v2 form follow the schema, and they are not verified (open question Q1). | spec.editable, spec.liveNow, spec.preload | editable, liveNow, id, version, schemaVersion, iteration, gnetId, __inputs, __requires, __elements | dropped | none | Metadata. | | none | __inputs item with type = datasource that --source-map names | changed | none | The item leaves the document. Each reference ${name} to it in a member datasource gets the mapped source (15.9). Another item of __inputs is metadata. | | metadata, other members | meta, other members | dropped | none | Metadata: resource version, generation, times, labels, other annotations. | | spec.variables | templating.list | see below | variables (4.7 to 4.11) | |

An absent or null value is changed to h. Another value is unsupported; fallback: no repeat.
maxPerRow of a panelmaxPerRowmappedrepeat.max_per_rowA whole number from 1 to 24 with direction h maps. With direction v the value is metadata, because Grafana ignores it. A value outside 1 to 24 is unsupported; fallback: member removed.
A panel with repeat that shares a row unit with another panelthe sameunsupportednoneGrafana moves the other panels. Hune needs free rows for the direction h (4.13a, rule 5). Fallback: no repeat. The report says "no repeat: the panel shares a row unit with another panel".
A panel with repeat in a row that has repeat, a panel with repeat that is a reuse panelthe sameunsupportednoneHune has one level of repeat and no repeat of a reuse panel (4.13a, rules 7 and 8). Fallback: no repeat on the panel. The repeat of the row stays.
More than 200 panels in all the sections, after the panels that the importer removesthe sameunsupportednone4.13, rule 2. Fallback: the dashboard is not imported. The importer then does not do the checks of the repeat across panels (the two rows above), because they compare the panels in pairs. The report says "the dashboard has N panels".
repeatIteration, repeatPanelId, repeatedByRow, scopedVarsthe samedroppednoneMetadata. Grafana writes them in the copies that it makes. A panel that has repeatPanelId is a stored copy: the importer removes it, and the report lists it.
spec.layout of kind TabsLayout or AutoGridLayoutnoneunsupportednoneFallback: the dashboard is not imported.

The v1 y rule: a panel that follows a row panel at y = r gets grid.y = y - r - 1. A panel before the first row keeps its y. A collapsed v1 row holds its panels in its member panels. The same rule applies to them. In the v2 form, y is already relative to its row.

A row whose panels have the smallest y equal to r is an exception. Grafana draws such panels on the first line of the section (measured on Grafana 12.4.2). Then each panel of that row gets grid.y = y - r, and the row has one item of class changed. The order of the panels and the overlap rules of 4.14 do not change. A panel with a y below r stays unsupported; fallback: the dashboard is not imported.

E.vizConfig.spec.fieldConfig.defaults.custom.wrapHeaderTextthe same memberdroppednoneMetadata when false. Else it is unsupported; fallback: property removed. The header of Hune never wraps (9.20).
E.vizConfig.spec.options.footer of a table (Grafana 11 and older)options.footerchangedthe property footer (4.18)Metadata when show is not true. Else the importer moves the footer as Grafana 12.4.2 moves it, with one changed item. The ids of reducer give footer.reducers by the row custom.footer of the field properties. When fields is absent, "" or [], the property goes in fields.defaults. One name in fields gives an override with the matcher name. Two names or more give the matcher names with the mode include. With countRows = true and count as the first reducer, the property is footer.reducers = ["count"] in fields.defaults. Then Grafana shows the row count in the first column only, and Hune shows it in each column. Another value of fields, or more than 64 different names, is unsupported; fallback: footer removed. The member enablePagination = true is unsupported, also when show is not true: Hune has no pages of rows. Its fallback: the panel shows all the rows.
E.linkslinks (panel links)mappedlinks (4.13, 4.23)targetBlank gives target_blank. A URL that breaks the prefix rule of 4.23, or a token outside 9.18, is unsupported; fallback: link removed. The importer removes each U+FEFF of the URL, with no item: the link editor of Grafana writes it around a token, and Grafana 12.4.2 makes the link without it.
E.vizConfig type text, options.mode = markdowntype, options.modemappedtype = text, options.mode = markdown (4.33a)The same rows apply in the v2 forms. A text panel has no query in Hune.
options.mode absent, or another text of Grafana that is not html or codesamechangedoptions.mode = markdownGrafana reads such a mode as markdown. The item is listed.
options.contentsamemappedoptions.content, the lines (4.33a)The text is cut at \n. A text with \r\n or \r is changed: each one becomes a line end. A control character other than U+0009 and U+000A is unsupported; fallback: the character is removed. A text above a limit of 4.35 is unsupported; fallback: panel removed.
options.content absentsamechangedoptions.content = []Grafana shows its default text. Hune shows an empty panel. The item is listed.

options.mode = htmlsameunsupportedoptions.mode = markdown, with the HTML as a code blockHune has no HTML mode (4.33a, rule 4). Fallback: the content becomes a code block, so the page shows the HTML as text. The fence has 3 backticks, or one more than the longest run of backticks in the content. It has no info string.
options.mode = codesamechangedoptions.mode = markdown, with the content as a code blockThe fence follows the rule of the row above. Hune does not colour the code.

E.data.spec.transformationstransformationssee belowtransforms (4.25)

The constructs of the sixth row of the table are these. The scan is a text scan of the lines, so it can list a construct inside a code block. That is a known limit of the report.

  • A < followed by a letter, / or !: HTML shows as text.
  • ![: no image shows. The page shows ! and a link.
  • & followed by letters or #, and then ;: an entity shows as written.
  • ~~: the marks of a strike show.
  • An item that starts with [ ] or [x]: the marks of a task show.
  • A line of = or - under a line of text: a setext heading shows as text or as a rule.
  • A line that starts with 4 spaces or a tab, after a blank line: an indented code block shows as text.
  • A line that starts with [name]:: a link reference shows as text. [text][name] and [^name] show as written.
  • A link whose destination breaks the rule of 9.19b. Examples are a scheme other than http and https, a start with //, a parenthesis and a character that is not printable ASCII. A www. start and a link title are in this item too.
  • A list or a quote nested more than 6 levels, or a table with more than 20 columns. This is unsupported; fallback: panel removed.
  • A placeholder with a format other than text, csv, pipe or glob. Also [[name]], a name that starts with __, and a placeholder in a destination.

Value tables of the options. A row gives a Grafana value and the value of format 2. A Grafana value that a table does not name follows the rule of the options row above. Some values have the same text in Grafana and in format 2, for example drawStyle line, bars and points. Such a value maps as it is and has no row here.

v2v1ClassHuneReason or fallback
E.data.spec.queries with one itemtargets with one itemmappeddata with the member query or reuse (4.15)
E.data.spec.queries with 2 to 8 itemstargets with 2 to 8 targetsmappeddata.query (the first item) and data.extra_queries (the other items in order) (4.15a)The rows below map each item. The type of the panel MUST be a type of 4.15a, rule 7. For a barchart or an xychart, the first item stays and the others are unsupported; fallback: items removed.
more than 8 itemsmore than 8 targetsunsupportednone4.15a, rule 5. Fallback: the items from the 9th are removed.
Q.hidden = truehide = truemappedhide = true of the queryThe query stays in the document and does not run (4.15a, rule 3). The member hide = false or an absent member gives no member. The numbers 1 and 0 are the same as true and false, and null is the same as an absent member. Another value, for example the text "true" or the number 2, is unsupported. Fallback: the query keeps hide = true and does not run, because Grafana hides such a target. A target of a panel that reuses another panel has no hide: the value true is changed, and the panel shows the frame of its source.
Q.refIdrefIdmappedrefA refId of the grammar of 4.3 maps as it is. The ref A of the first query gives no member. A refId that breaks the grammar, or that two queries share, or an absent refId, is changed: the importer writes the first free letter of A to Z and lists the item. A panel with one query writes no ref, whatever its refId, so a document that the import makes with one query per panel has the same bytes as before (4.42). The refId of such a panel is metadata.

data source of type grafana-clickhouse-datasource with another uidsameunsupportednoneFallback: panel removed.

Field members (fieldConfig in v1, E.vizConfig.spec.fieldConfig in v2):

A Grafana property is a member of defaults or an item of overrides[].properties (its id and value). The same row applies to both places. In defaults, the result goes to fields.defaults. In an override, it goes to fields.overrides[].set. Both are property sets of 4.18.

Grafana property (defaults member or override id)ClassHune member of the property set (4.18)Reason or fallback
unitmappedunit (4.20)A unit of 4.20 maps. The Grafana custom units suffix:<text> and prefix:<text> map as they are. Another unit is unsupported (19.16); fallback: property removed.
decimalsmappeddecimals
min, maxmappedmin, max
displayNamemappeddisplay_name19.19.
noValuemappedno_valueOnly in fields.defaults (4.18). In an override, it is unsupported; fallback: property removed.
thresholdsmappedthresholds (4.21)Modes absolute and percentage. Grafana reads the first step as the base step, whatever its value. The first step of Hune has the value null, so an export with the value 0 in the first step gives null.
colormappedcolor (4.19)Grafana color.mode fixed gives mode = fixed, thresholds gives thresholds, and palette-classic gives palette. The ids palette-classic-by-name and palette-saturated also give palette, each with one changed item. The ten ids continuous-GrYlRd, continuous-RdYlGr, continuous-BlYlRd, continuous-YlRd, continuous-BlPu, continuous-YlBl, continuous-blues, continuous-reds, continuous-greens and continuous-purples give the modes continuous_gr_yl_rd, continuous_rd_yl_gr, continuous_bl_yl_rd, continuous_yl_rd, continuous_bl_pu, continuous_yl_bl, continuous_blues, continuous_reds, continuous_greens and continuous_purples. Another mode (shades) is unsupported; fallback: property removed. Grafana color.fixedColor gives fixed with the mode fixed, and null with the other modes. A colour that is not a token, a hex colour or an rgb colour of 4.19 is changed to the nearest token. Grafana color.seriesBy is dropped (listed when it is not last). Hune has one series palette, the 18 colours of ClickHouse Dash (9.16). The mode palette draws it, not the classic palette of Grafana. This difference is wanted (19.20a), and the importer does not list it for palette-classic. The other palette modes lose their own rule (a colour by series name, a saturated list): the item text names the Grafana mode. The explicit colours, the thresholds, the mappings and the continuous modes do not change.
mappingsmappedmappings (4.22)The split and order rule below.
linksmappedlinks (4.23)targetBlank gives target_blank. A URL that breaks the prefix rule of 4.23, or a token outside 9.18, is unsupported; fallback: link removed. The importer removes each U+FEFF of the URL, as for a panel link.

custom.alignmappedalignauto, left, center, right. Only in a table panel.
custom.cellOptions, older custom.displayModemappedcellType auto, color-text and color-background give auto, color_text and color_background, with the mode gradient or basic. For color-background, an absent mode gives gradient, and another mode is unsupported; fallback: gradient. In the older custom.displayMode, color-background gives color_background with gradient, and color-background-solid gives color_background with basic. Type gauge with mode basic, gradient or lcd gives gauge with this mode. An absent mode gives gradient, and another mode is unsupported; fallback: gradient. In the older custom.displayMode, basic, gradient-gauge and lcd-gauge give gauge with basic, gradient and lcd. The type json-view (older and newer form) gives json. Another cell type is unsupported (19.15); fallback: auto. Only in a table panel.

custom.footer (reducers)mappedfooterAn empty reducers is metadata. Else each id gives a value of reduce.calc (the table above). countAll gives count. Another id is unsupported; fallback: the item is removed. An item that repeats a calculation is changed (removed). More than 8 different items: each item after the eighth is unsupported; fallback: the item is removed, so the first 8 stay. A footer object with no reducers, or custom.footer.reducers as an array, are read in the same way. A value that is not an array is unsupported; fallback: property removed. Only in a table panel.

In a table panel, the member viz gives hidden (Grafana 12 hides a column with hideFrom.viz), and legend and tooltip have no equal. A flag that is not a boolean is unsupported; fallback: property removed.

The member dash gives line_dash_array when it differs from the default of Hune: [10, 10] for dash and [0, 10] for dot. Grafana draws [10, 10] for a fill with no dash, also for dot, and dashes for a dash with no fill: the importer writes these arrays. An array that is not 1 to 8 integers from 0 to 100, one above 0, is unsupported; fallback: the default array of the fill. Only in a timeseries.

Another value is unsupported; fallback: property removed.

another matcher (byType, byValue)unsupportednoneFallback: override removed.

The split and order rule of the value mappings. A Grafana property mappings is an array of items. An item of type value holds many values in options: one key for each value, each with a text, a color and an index. Items of type range, regex and special hold one options.result with text, color and index.

  1. Each key of a value item gives one mapping of kind value (4.22), with value equal to the key.
  2. A range item gives one mapping of kind range (options.from, options.to). A regex item gives one mapping of kind regex (options.pattern). A special item gives one mapping of kind special.
  3. The Grafana special matches null, nan, null+nan, true, false and empty give null, nan, null_nan, true, false and empty.
  4. The importer puts the mappings in the order of index, from the lowest. Two equal index values keep the order of the input. This is the order in which Grafana tries them.
  5. An empty text gives null. An absent color gives null. A mapping whose text and color are both null is dropped (listed).
  6. More than MAX_MAPPINGS (4.35) mappings is unsupported; fallback: the first MAX_MAPPINGS mappings stay.

Transformations (each item of transformations). A v1 item is {id, options, disabled, filter}. A v2 item is {kind, spec}: kind is the Grafana id, and spec holds id, options, disabled and filter. The table names the id and the members without the prefix spec.. More than 16 items give unsupported; fallback: the first 16 items stay, as for MAX_TRANSFORMS (4.35).

Grafana idClassHuneReason or fallback
filterByValuemappedtransforms item of kind filter_by_value (4.25)options.type (include, exclude) gives mode. options.match (any, all) gives match. Each filter gives field (from fieldName) and matcher. The Grafana matcher ids equal, notEqual, greater, greaterOrEqual, lower, lowerOrEqual, between, regex, isNull and isNotNull give the kinds equal, not_equal, greater, greater_or_equal, lower, lower_or_equal, range, regex, is_null and is_not_null. Another matcher (for example substring, notSubstring) is unsupported; fallback: transformation removed. M0 counts them (18.3).

organizemappedtransforms item of kind organize (4.25a)excludeByName (the names with true) gives hide. indexByName gives order, the names sorted by index. A tie of two indexes is changed: the names keep the order of the keys of the file. renameByName gives rename (an empty new name is dropped). A non-empty includeByName is unsupported; fallback: the member is dropped and listed, and the other members stay. More than 64 names in a list is unsupported; fallback: transformation removed.
calculateFieldmappedkind calculate_fieldmode binary, reduceRow, unary and index are mapped (reduceRow gives reduce_row). alias (an empty text gives null) and replaceFields are mapped. For binary, an operand that is a number text, {fixed: "2"} or a byName matcher gives the operand number or field. Another operator than +, -, * and /, an operand with $, an empty operand, a fixed text that is not a number, a matcher byType, and two number operands are unsupported; fallback: transformation removed. The reduce.reducer ids map to the calculations of 4.26 (sum gives total, lastNotNull gives last_not_null, stdDev gives std_dev, diffperc gives diff_percent, distinctCount gives distinct_count, changeCount gives change_count). Another reducer (allValues, p50) is unsupported; fallback: transformation removed. ignoreNulls and nullValueMode with a value other than null as zero are dropped (no effect, measured). nullValueMode null as zero is unsupported; fallback: transformation removed. The modes cumulativeFunctions and windowFunctions, and an absent mode, are unsupported; fallback: transformation removed. The member timeSeries is dropped.
sortBymappedkind sort_bysort gives sort: field and desc. More than 8 keys is unsupported; fallback: the first 8 keys. reverse is dropped (no effect, measured). A key that equals an earlier key is dropped. An empty sort gives no transform; the item is dropped.
limitmappedkind limitlimitField as a number or a number text: the whole part of the absolute value gives count, and a negative value gives end = last. An absent, empty or non-number value is dropped (no effect, measured). A text with $ is unsupported; fallback: transformation removed. A value above 1,000,000 is unsupported; fallback: count = 1,000,000.
mergemappedkind mergeNo option.
reducemappedkind reducemode (seriesToRows or absent gives series_to_rows, reduceFields gives reduce_fields) and reducers (absent gives ["max"]) are mapped, with the calculation ids of the row calculateField. An empty list of reducers is dropped (no effect, measured). A reducer that has no calculation is unsupported; fallback: the item of the list is removed, and with no reducer left the transformation is removed. A non-empty fields is unsupported; fallback: transformation removed (Grafana 12.4.2 gives a panel error, measured). includeTimeField and labelsToFields are dropped.
partitionByValueschangedkind partition_by_valuesfields (1 to 4 names; another count is unsupported; fallback: transformation removed) and keepFields are mapped. Hune splits each frame and Grafana splits the first frame only (19.20j), so the transformation has one item with the class changed. naming.asLabels equal to false is changed: Hune always makes labels. naming.append and the separators are dropped.
joinByField, seriesToColumnsmappedkind join_by_fieldbyField (an empty or absent value gives null) and mode (outer or absent, inner) are mapped. The mode outerTabular is unsupported; fallback: transformation removed.
filterFieldsByNamemappedkind filter_fields_by_nameinclude and exclude give names and pattern. A pattern that 4.24 refuses is unsupported; fallback: transformation removed. The members byVariable and variable are unsupported; fallback: transformation removed.
renameByRegexmappedkind rename_by_regexregex and renamePattern give regex and rename_pattern. A regex that 4.24 refuses is unsupported; fallback: transformation removed. An absent regex is dropped with the item.
convertFieldTypechangedkind convert_field_typeEach conversion gives field and to (string gives text). The destination types enum and other, and a conversion with dateFormat, joinWith or enumConfig, are unsupported; fallback: the conversion is removed, and with none left the transformation is removed. A second conversion of the same field, and a conversion after the 16th, are unsupported; fallback: the conversion is removed. The rules of the values differ (19.20j), so the class is changed.
