16.6 Store conformance suite

The store conformance suite is the suite of 5.33 to 5.36. Section 5 owns its cases: the common tests of 5.34 (SC-), the volume tests of 5.35 (SV-) and the PostgreSQL tests of 5.36 (SP-). Its rules are those of 5.33.

The store job (16.17) runs the common tests on a volume in a temporary directory, and from M5 on PostgreSQL 15 and 18 in containers. It runs the volume tests on a tmpfs, with a tmpfs of 1 MiB for the test of a full disk. It runs the PostgreSQL tests with a TCP proxy in front of PostgreSQL for the tests that break a connection. Each PostgreSQL database of the job has the two roles of 5.25: migrate-store uses the owner role, and the suite uses the application role.

16.7 Cases added to the store suite

CaseBackendCheck
SX-01volumeAn fsync error (fault injection in the test build): the save fails with Unavailable, the temporary file is removed, and the backend calls fsync no second time (5.13).
SX-02storesStores (5.42 to 5.44): 14 tests in crates/hune-store/tests/stores.rs. The first load and the merge, the refusal of each write into a read-only store, the ids that two stores cannot share, each start rule, the primary store with no dashboard, the last valid state of a file that becomes not valid, a file that is new and not valid, the period of each store, and a write in the primary store that the catalog sees at once.

16.8 API black-box suite

The suite is the integration test api of the crate hune. It sends HTTP requests to the binary on the compose network and reads nothing inside the process. The compose project holds these services:

  • the binary of the build;
  • a ClickHouse of the pinned LTS version, with seed data. At its first start it runs tests/fixtures/clickhouse/init.sql. This file is the SQL of 8.12 that creates the profile and the user hune. It names the test database analytics, not the production database. The test configuration names this user, with password_file;
  • PostgreSQL 18, with the two roles of 5.25 (from M5, 16.17);
  • a second PostgreSQL 18 database analytics for a postgres source, with the role hune_ro of 8a.13 (from the milestone PG). It runs tests/fixtures/postgres-source/init.sql (16.9a);
  • fake-pdp of the pinned ProcurAuth commit.

The cases A01 to A38 run four times: volume in mode open, PostgreSQL in mode open, and the same two with no primary store. The last two runs check the cases that name the primary store. The runs on PostgreSQL start at M5. The cases A39 to A45 run on the volume in mode authzen with fake-pdp, from M6. Their configuration is tests/api/authzen.hcl: the audience hune-test, the sources prod and conf, and cache_ttl = "30s".

CaseCheck
A01GET /api/dashboards lists each valid document, and each invalid document with its errors.
A02GET /api/dashboards/{uid}?download=true gives the canonical bytes, whose SHA-256 is the etag. The plain GET /api/dashboards/{uid} gives the answer of 6.14 with the member document. Both have the etag in the ETag header.
A03GET of an unknown uid in mode open: 404.
A04POST /api/dashboards creates the document in its folder. A uid that exists: 409.
A05PUT /api/dashboards/{uid} with the current If-Match and a new Idempotency-Key: a new etag and one history version.
A06PUT without If-Match: refused, no change.
A07PUT with an old If-Match: 409 with the current etag, no change.
A08PUT repeated with the same Idempotency-Key and body: the same answer, one version.
A09PUT with a used Idempotency-Key and another body: refused, no change.
A10PUT of a document whose uid differs from the path: refused, with the JSON pointer /uid.
A11PUT of a document with three errors: the answer lists the three errors with their JSON pointers.
A12PUT of a document with a SQL text that starts with another keyword than SELECT or WITH, or with two statements: refused, with the pointer of the SQL.
A13DELETE /api/dashboards/{uid}: a tombstone. The next GET gives 404. The history stays.
A14GET /api/dashboards/{uid}/history gives the versions, newest first. GET .../history/{n} gives the bytes of version n.
A15POST /api/dashboards/{uid}/restore: a new version with the bytes of the chosen version.
A16POST /api/dashboards/{uid}/move: same uid, new folder. A folder that does not exist: refused.
A17GET and POST /api/folders, PUT and DELETE /api/folders/{id}: create, title change, delete of an empty folder. A folder with documents: refused.
A18POST /api/dashboards/{uid}/panels/{id}/query with the current etag, an absolute range, max_data_points and variable values: a frame of section 7 with the expected columns and kinds.
A19The same query with an old etag: 409.
A20A query with from >= to, with max_data_points out of range, or for an unknown panel: refused. A query for a panel of kind reuse: refused.
A21A variable value that section 7 refuses: refused, and system.query_log of the test ClickHouse shows no new query.
A22POST /api/dashboards/{uid}/variables/{name}/options: the options. A variable that depends on another variable gets its options with the value of the other.
A23GET /api/sources: ids and labels, and no URL, user or secret.
A24POST /api/sources/{id}/preview runs draft SQL. With no primary store: refused.
A25GET /api/session: the mode, the subject (- in mode open) and the editing flag.
A26With no primary store, each write route is refused and each read route works. A write into a read-only store gives 403 read_only_store (11.35).
A27Write guard: Sec-Fetch-Site: cross-site is refused. No Sec-Fetch-Site and an Origin of another host is refused. A body that is not application/json is refused. An OPTIONS request gets no CORS header.
A28A body above server.max_request_bytes is refused before the server reads all of it. A header block above 16 KiB is refused. A connection above server.max_connections is refused or waits, as section 14 says.
A29Each class of answer (shell, static file, API, error) has the security headers of section 12, with the exact CSP text.
A30/static/{build-hash}/... has the immutable cache header. Another build hash gives 404. The shell has no-cache.
A31With server.base_path = "/dashboards/", each route answers under the prefix, and the shell holds relative links only.
A32Mode open: /.well-known/procurauth/actions and /.well-known/procurauth/resources give 404.
A33Ops listener: /livez gives 200. /readyz gives 503 before the first load of the catalog and 200 after it. /metrics gives the text format with the prefix hune_. These paths give 404 on the main listener. The API paths give 404 on the ops listener.
A34After the first load, stop PostgreSQL and ClickHouse: /readyz stays 200, GET /api/dashboards/{uid} gives 200 from the catalog, and a save fails with a retryable error.
A35SIGTERM during a slow query: /readyz gives 503 at once. During SHUTDOWN_DRAIN (5 s), new connections are served as usual (6.28, 14.13). After it, new connections are refused, and a request on an open connection gets 503 server_stopping. The process exits with 0 within server.shutdown_grace plus SHUTDOWN_DRAIN. When this time ends first, the exit code is 1.
A36Each log line on the standard output is one JSON object with event. No line holds a SQL text, a secret or a token: the test searches the whole log for the secret values and for a marker in the SQL.
A37No metric label holds a uid, a subject or a SQL text: the test compares the label values with the fixture uids.
A38The variables of K14, set to bad values, change no answer of the suite.
A39Mode authzen: a request without a token gets 401 on each route, except /.well-known/procurauth/actions.
A40An unknown uid: the PDP answers false for dashboard.read on the service, so the answer is 403. The PDP answers true, so the answer is 404.
A41The PDP does not answer: 503 pdp_unavailable, retryable. A kept answer that is younger than cache_ttl still applies.
A42The ceilings max_rows and max_duration of the answer lower the settings of the query. system.query_log shows the lower max_result_rows and max_execution_time for the query_id.
A43A save asks source.query on each source of the new document, with no usage. A false answer refuses the save.
A44The list shows only the folders and dashboards that get true for dashboard.read with subtree.
A45GET /.well-known/procurauth/actions gives a JSON value equal to the golden file tests/golden/api/action-document.json: the document of 11.18 for tests/api/authzen.hcl. The test compares JSON values, not bytes.
A46Bodies in flight (6.27, 12.16): while MAX_INFLIGHT_DOC_BODIES (8) large bodies are in flight, one more create or save gets 503 server_busy. While MAX_INFLIGHT_SMALL_BODIES (256) small bodies are in flight, one more small request with a body gets 503 server_busy. When the bodies end, the next request succeeds. The test holds the bodies open with slow uploads.
A47The milestone PG. A panel query on a postgres source gives a frame with the expected columns and kinds (7.16a). A dashboard with one panel on each kind of source works.
A48GET /api/sources lists the postgres source with kind postgres and its state, and no host, user or secret.
A49A save, a restore and a preview with SQL for a postgres source that breaks 7.25 (a DELETE, two statements, pg_sleep, $1, FOR UPDATE) are refused, with the pointer of the SQL. The same text on a clickhouse source follows 7.12.
A50Mode authzen: the ceilings max_rows and max_duration lower the limits of a PostgreSQL query. A result above the ceiling gives 403 ceiling_exceeded.
A51The log holds no SQL text and no value of a PostgreSQL query. query.run has source_kind postgres and the field sqlstate. The action document holds the postgres source in resources.
A52The milestone MMG. A panel query on a mongodb source gives a frame with the expected columns and kinds (7.16c). A dashboard with one panel on each of the three kinds of source works.
A53GET /api/sources lists the mongodb source with kind mongodb and its state, and no host, user, database, collection or secret.
A54A save, a restore and a preview with a query for a mongodb source that breaks 7.28 to 7.33 are refused, with the pointer of the line (7.36). The set holds $out, $merge, $where, $function, a $lookup outside the list, a duplicate key, JavaScript text, and a placeholder in a key. The same text on a clickhouse source follows 7.12.
A55Mode authzen: the ceilings max_rows and max_duration lower the limits of a MongoDB query. A result above the ceiling gives 403 ceiling_exceeded.
A56The log holds no query text and no value of a MongoDB query. query.run has source_kind mongodb and the field mongo_code. The action document holds the mongodb source in resources.
A60The parameter tag of 6.12. One dashboard has the tag chain and one has not. tag=chain lists the first. tag=other lists none. A tag of 65 bytes, an empty tag and two tag give 400 bad_request. tag with q and folder keeps the dashboards that pass all. Mode authzen: a dashboard that the subject cannot see is not in the list, with or without tag.
A61The stores (5.42 to 5.44, 6.10 to 6.14, 11.35). 15 tests in crates/hune/src/server/tests/stores.rs. The session lists the stores. The list, the folders and the read give read_only, store and stale. Each write into a read-only store gives 403 read_only_store and changes nothing, in the mode open and in the mode authzen. Hune never asks dashboard.write for it. A subject with no right gets the same bytes for a target of a read-only store and for one that does not exist. Each start rule stops the start with the label, the file and the reason. A file that becomes not valid keeps its last valid state, with the warning, the log line and the gauge.
A70Several queries (6.20, 4.15a). A panel with 3 queries answers 3 requests with ref, one for each query, each with its own query_id. A request with no ref runs the first query. A ref that no query has gives 404 not_found. A body with an SQL member, a source member or a ref of a wrong type gives 400.
A71Hidden queries (6.20, T65). A request for a hidden query gives 400 bad_request, with the ref and for a hidden first query with no ref. The log has no query.run line, and no query reaches the source. A panel with all queries hidden is a valid document.
A72The save (7.18, 11.21). A save, a restore, an import and a validate_only check each query of a panel. A fault in the third query gives the pointer /sections/0/panels/<n>/data/extra_queries/1/sql/<line>. 8 queries are valid and 9 give out_of_range. A document with 401 queries gives too_large. A panel with repeat and extra_queries gives not_permitted.
A73Mode authzen, with fake-pdp. A save of a panel whose second query names the source conf asks source.query on conf, also when the query is hidden. A subject with source.query on prod only gets 403 forbidden. A viewer with dashboard.read runs each query of the panel, and each request asks one question with the same key, so the library makes one call with cache_ttl = "30s". With cache_ttl = "0s" each request asks its own question. A subject without dashboard.read gets 403 for a hidden ref and for an unknown ref.
A74Mixed sources. A panel with a ClickHouse query and a PostgreSQL query, and a panel with a ClickHouse query and a MongoDB query, run each query with the rules of its kind (7.19, 7.35). A hostile free value gives 422 variable_value_refused for the second query, and the source gets no byte (T66). A query with a refused statement gives 422 sql_invalid and the other queries of the panel answer. A query above max_query_size of its own source gives 422 sql_too_large for that query only.
A75Limits (8.8). A panel with queries on two sources, with max_concurrent_per_subject = 1 and a short queue_wait: each source counts its own queries, and the query that waits too long gives 503 source_busy alone. 8 requests of one panel at the same time, with max_concurrent_per_subject = 2, give 2 answers and 6 503 source_busy. A refused query never reaches its source.
A90A closed custom variable (the member absent, then false): a value outside the options, sent to 6.20, 6.21 (as a parent), 6.21a and 6.22, gives 422 variable_value_refused. system.query_log of the test ClickHouse shows no new query.
A91The same for a list with one item outside the options, for the text of an option in place of its value, and for variants of case, blank and Unicode form.
A92An open custom variable: a hostile value gives a literal in a string, a number in code, or 422. The log of the source shows the escaped text.
A93A save with an open custom variable in a quoted name gives 422 invalid_document with not_permitted at the line of the SQL. The same text with a closed variable is saved.
A94A request for an open custom variable with 0 bytes, 1 byte, 1,024 bytes, 1,025 bytes and a control character: 422 for the first, the fourth and the fifth.
A95An open custom variable on a MongoDB source: the value $secret gives 422, and the value secret runs.
A96A query variable with the member true and with false: a value outside the options gives the same answer, byte for byte, on 6.20, 6.21 as a parent, 6.21a and 6.22.
A97The time of the check of a closed custom variable with 10,000 values and 10,000 options is 250 ms or less.
A98A document with allow_custom_value on a text variable, with a text as value, and with a number as value: PUT ...?validate_only=true gives unknown_member and wrong_type at /variables/<i>/allow_custom_value.
A76The heatmap and the histogram (4.33c, 4.33d, T69). A save of a document with each type gives 200 and the canonical bytes. The options at their boundaries are valid, and each one out of range by one gives 422 invalid_document with its pointer. A panel query of each type answers as for a table, and the server never buckets. A result of 100,000 rows of counts passes without a cut. The server of the previous version, which the suite starts as a second binary, refuses a document with each type with bad_format at type.
A103A document with the two kinds is saved and read back with the same canonical bytes (4.38). The etag is the same for the same bytes.
A104Each of 20 invalid variables of 4.10a and 4.10b gives 422 invalid_document with the code and the pointer of 4.37.
A105A panel query with an interval value that is an item gives 200 on the four routes. The values $__auto with auto false, an item with a space, 7m that is not an item, an empty list, 2 values and all: true give 422 variable_value_refused. A hostile set of 40 texts gives 422 and no source query.
A106A panel query for a constant: no member gives 200, the equal member gives 200, a different member and all: true give 422, and no source query runs for a refusal.
A107The text of "Auto" for a range of 6 h, 5 min and 30 d, with auto_count 30 and auto_min 10s, is 10m, 10s and 1d. The same text goes to all the routes of the request.
A108The route of 6.21 with a query variable whose SQL names an interval and a constant. A $__interval_s in that SQL keeps its error (7.18, item 5).
A109The annotation route (6.21a) with a SQL that names a constant and an interval gives the marks. The test covers it with the four routes of A105 and A106.
A110The preview (6.22) with dashboard_variables that holds the two kinds runs a draft panel and a draft variable. A constant is read from the body and not from variables.
A111The import of a Grafana dashboard with an interval and a constant gives the rows of 15.6. A dashboard with 1M in the list gives the report row and the saved document has the other items.
A140The query parameters of ClickHouse (7.6a). A panel query, an options query, an annotation query and a preview with {name:Type} give 200. The source receives the SQL with the forms and no value, and the pairs param_<name> after log_comment, in the byte order of the names. Other values give the same SQL (PQ). The member names of 6.14 names the variables of the forms.
A141The refusals of 7.6a send no byte to the source. A value outside the grammar of its type, 1,001 items and pairs above 4,096 bytes give 422 variable_value_refused, with a reason that names the type and holds no value. A form that a constant makes in the final text gives 422 sql_invalid. A save with each fault of 7.18, items 10 to 15, gives 422 invalid_document with the code and the line. A status 414 of a proxy gives 422 query_error with clickhouse_code = null, and the source stays up.
A100A document with one transform of each of the eleven kinds is saved and read back with the same canonical bytes (4.38). The etag is the same for the same bytes.
A101Each of 20 invalid transforms of 4.25a gives 422 invalid_document with the code and the pointer of 4.37 (unknown_member, missing_member, wrong_type, out_of_range, too_large, not_unique, bad_format, not_permitted). No other error stops the list.
A102The import of a Grafana dashboard with the 11 ids gives the rows of 15.6: the classes, the fallbacks and the report. The saved document has the new kinds and the same bytes again after a second save.

16.9 ClickHouse integration cases

The source job runs these cases on two ClickHouse versions: the version that 19.4 names and the latest LTS release. Each version has a tag and a digest in tests/images.lock. Each test ClickHouse runs tests/fixtures/clickhouse/init.sql at its first start (16.8): the profile and the user of 8.12, with the test database analytics.

CaseCheck
C01Each macro of section 7, expanded and run on a seed table with known times, gives the expected rows.
C02$__timeInterval gives the expected bucket boundaries for each step of the interval table.
C03For 2,000 random values of each CI run (16.4), SELECT <value as a substituted literal> returns the value, unchanged.
C04A result above max_result_rows, a result above max_result_bytes, a run above max_execution_time and a SQL text above max_query_size each give their error code of section 6. A cut result is never shown as complete.
C05Cancellation: the test starts a panel query that runs 30 s, then closes its client connection. system.processes has no row for the query_id within 2 s.
C06The read-only guard of section 8: an account with readonly = 0, 1 and 2 each gives the behaviour of section 8.
C07Each ClickHouse type of section 8 gives its frame kind and value: UInt64 maximum and Int128 as exact text, Decimal(38, 9) as exact text, DateTime64(3) and Date as Unix seconds, Nullable, LowCardinality, Bool, NaN and infinities.
C08system.query_log holds the query_id and the log_comment of section 8 for each query.
C09Account contract: each CHECK GRANT line and the SHOW GRANTS line of 8.12 give the value that 8.12 writes beside them, with the test database analytics in place of the production database.
C10TLS with the test CA succeeds. A wrong tls.server_name stops the connection. No system CA store is read.
C11A ClickHouse that is down: the panel query fails with a retryable error, /readyz stays 200, and the source state is down.
C12A SETTINGS clause in the SQL gives an error of ClickHouse, and the query does not run, in each of these cases: a value above a constraint of the profile; result_overflow_mode = 'break'; timeout_overflow_mode = 'break'; read_overflow_mode = 'break' (the CONST constraints of 8.12).
C13After 15 s without a query, the next query succeeds on the first attempt: the client never sends on a connection that ClickHouse closed (its keep_alive_timeout is 10 s).
C14Two queries of one panel. The test sends two requests with ref. system.query_log holds two rows with two query_id, and the log_comment of each has its ref (8.10). A client that closes the connection of the first request leaves the second query running, and the first query leaves system.processes within 2 s.
C20An open custom variable with the value x' OR 1=1 -- in a string: ClickHouse receives one query with the escaped literal, and the result has no row.
C21A closed custom variable with a value outside the options: no query reaches ClickHouse. The options query of a query variable keeps 10,000 rows (a result of 10,001 rows gives 422 query_limit) with the member true and with false.
C22A query variable with a value outside its options runs as free text: ClickHouse receives the query with the literal, and the answer is the same as for a text variable.
C15The queries of the heatmap and of the histogram (9.19e, 9.19f). Three panel queries run on the seed table: the buckets of intDiv(latency, 10) * 10 with count(), the columns of histogram(10)(latency) taken apart with tupleElement as xMin, xMax and count, and a wide frame of countIf for each bucket. The columns count() and UInt64 have the kind number_text, and the page reads their double. The test saves the three frames as the goldens of FE-44 and FE-46. A version that has no histogram function skips that query with a message.
C30parseTimeDelta('${bucket}') with an interval value 1m, 1d, 1w, 1y and 500ms in a string runs on the server and gives 60, 86,400, 604,800, 31,536,000 and 0.5.
C31The text of "Auto" in a string runs for 6 ranges, and the statement returns the table text of 7.4a.
C32A constant with the hostile text a'b"c$d\e;--x runs in a string (escaped, one row) and in a quoted name (escaped). In code the text stays as it is, and the final SQL check refuses it because it opens a string. The result has no second statement.
C33An interval request with a hostile value gives 422 and no query on the source.
C34A constant request with a different value gives 422 and no query on the source.
C35The seeded random tests of 16.4 with an interval and a constant in the document: each hostile value of a query or text variable still gives an error or keeps P.
C40The query parameters of 7.6a. For each type, SELECT of the parameter gives back each value of a hostile set: quote marks, backslashes, ], ,, Unicode, the escapes of ClickHouse as text, and texts of 1,024 bytes. The escapes of TAB, line feed, carriage return and zero give back the character. The values that the grammars refuse show the measure of 26.8: code 457 or 130 for a zero before the first digit, and a changed value for an integer out of range and for a date out of range. No answer has the code 456 or 457. a${t:Identifier} gives a column with the name of the value, so the second scan of 7.6a reads a { right after $.
C41After a query with a query parameter, system.query_log holds the statement with the value in place of the form, as for the text form of 7.6.

The cases C40 and C41 are ignored tests in the module f39 of crates/hune-source/tests/clickhouse.rs. The job source runs them with the other C cases. The cases A140 and A141 are tests of crates/hune/src/server/tests/query_params.rs with the fake source. The unit tests of 7.6a are in crates/hune-core/src/sql/params.rs and crates/hune-core/tests/f39_query_params.rs. They hold each loose form, the grammar of each type, the caps, the second scan and the name scan. They also check the property PQ with seeded random values.

The ad hoc filters (F36) have the ignored test adhoc_a_string_literal_compares_with_each_column_type in crates/hune-source/tests/adhoc_clickhouse.rs. The job source runs it with tests/ci/source.sh. The test makes a table with the types Int32, UInt64, Float64, Date, DateTime, Decimal(18, 4), Enum8, LowCardinality(String), Nullable(Int32) and Nullable(String). It runs $__adhocFilters with one filter for 27 cases, hostile values included, and compares the count of rows. It also records the code of ClickHouse for a text that does not convert (7.10a).

The cases C30, C31, C32 and C35 are ignored tests in the module f27 of crates/hune-source/tests/clickhouse.rs. The job source runs them with the other C cases. C30 also runs the 30 texts of the table of 7.4a. C31 uses the ranges 1 s, 5 min, 1 h, 6 h, 24 h and 7 d with auto_count 30 and auto_min 10s. C32 also sends a constant that tries to end its statement, and checks that the seed table keeps its 30 rows.

C35 runs 4 seeds of 500 cases. Set HUNE_TEST_SEED to run one seed again.

The cases C33 and C34 are ignored tests real_c33 and real_c34 in crates/hune/src/server/tests/real_ch.rs. The script tests/ci/server.sh runs them. Each test sends a good request first, and reads the SQL text of Hune in system.query_log. It counts the queries of Hune in the log before and after the refused requests. The two counts must be equal.

16.9a PostgreSQL source integration cases

The pgsource job runs these cases on PostgreSQL 15 and 18. Each version has a tag and a digest in tests/images.lock. Each test server runs in a disposable container on 127.0.0.1, with synthetic data only. At its first start it runs tests/fixtures/postgres-source/init.sql. This file holds these items:

  • the owner role, the database analytics and the schema metrics;
  • tables with one column of each type of 7.16a;
  • a table of 1,000,000 rows made with generate_series, and a table with a column of 50 MB;
  • the role hune_ro of 8a.13, with its revokes;
  • thirteen deviant roles for the checks R1 to R9. One is a member of a role that can write, without INHERIT (R4). One owns a table (R9). Three can run pg_terminate_backend, pg_logical_emit_message or ts_stat (R7). PS11 uses the roles for R1, R2, R3, R4, R7, R8 and R9;
    • one has CREATEROLE, one is a member of pg_read_all_data, and one has CREATE on a schema;
    • one has a write grant on a table, one has UPDATE on a sequence, and one has INSERT on one column;
    • one has EXECUTE on the functions of dblink, and one has USAGE on a foreign server;
  • the role hune_rw, which has all rights, for PS07;
  • the roles hune_md5 and hune_clear, which have the contract of hune_ro. Their login methods are md5 and password. PS12 uses them.

The file tests/fixtures/postgres-source/apply.sh runs init.sql at the first start. It gives the passwords to psql as variables, so init.sql holds no password.

The cases use three clients. The first is the binary of the build. The second is the client of the crate hune-source, called directly. It runs no statement check, because the check is in hune-core, so it sends any text. The third is a plain tokio-postgres client with no startup parameter of 8a.4.

A proxy on 127.0.0.1 sits between a client and the server in the cases that name it. It records each message of the protocol. In PS07 the test sets the state of the source to up by hand, because the probe refuses the role hune_rw.

The name of each test starts with its case id in lower case, for example ps04_row_limit_is_exact. The script tests/ci/pgsource.sh reports each case PS01 to PS15 as PASS, FAIL or NO TEST. It gives the connection to the tests in variables with the prefix HUNE_TEST_PGS_. The program of Hune reads none of them. A run with no network sets HUNE_PGS_NO_PULL=1, and the script then uses the local images postgres:15 and postgres:18. The script tests/fixtures/postgres-source/demo.sh makes a demo database from synthetic data, and it prints a postgres "demo" block for the configuration.

CaseCheck
PS01Each macro of 7.22, expanded and run on a seed table with known times, gives the expected rows. The vectors of 16.13 give the expected texts. $__timeGroupAlias(ts, $__interval) and $__timeFilter are in the set.
PS02$__timeGroup gives the expected bucket boundaries for each step of the interval table of 7.4 and for each unit of 7.23.
PS03For 2,000 random values of each run (16.4), SELECT <value as a substituted literal> returns the value, unchanged. The set holds a standard string, an E string, a quoted name and a list of literals. The values hold quote marks, backslashes, comment marks, $ and Unicode spaces. Each session has standard_conforming_strings = on.
PS04Limits. A result of exactly max_result_rows rows passes, and one more row gives 422 query_limit. A result above max_result_bytes gives its code. A query above max_execution_time gives 504 query_timeout, for one Execute message and for a query of many Execute messages. A SQL text above max_query_size gives 422 sql_too_large. A result with a row of 50 MB and max_result_bytes of 1 MiB gives 422 query_limit, and the client reads no second row. A cut result is never shown as complete.
PS05Cancellation. The test starts a query that runs 30 s, then closes its client connection. pg_stat_activity has no row with its application_name within 2 s. The same holds at the client deadline. When the proxy drops the cancel request, the row leaves within 3 s, because of client_connection_check_interval.
PS06Each type of 7.16a gives its kind and its value: the int8 maximum, numeric(38,9), numeric NaN as null, float4 and float8 with NaN and infinities, timestamptz with microseconds, timestamp, date, infinity, jsonb, arrays with NULL elements, an enum, a domain, inet. A column of each unsupported type gives 422 query_error with sqlstate null, and the proxy shows no Execute message.
PS07The connection side alone. The client of hune-source connects with the role hune_rw. Each text of INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE, CREATE TABLE, SELECT ... INTO and a data-changing CTE fails with the SQLSTATE 25006. After SELECT set_config('default_transaction_read_only', 'off', false) and after SET TRANSACTION READ WRITE, a write still fails with 25006. The next query on the pool shows default_transaction_read_only = on.
PS08The role side alone. The plain client logs in as hune_ro and opens BEGIN READ WRITE. Each statement of 8a.13 fails with the SQLSTATE that 8a.13 gives.
PS09One statement. The text SELECT 1; SELECT 2 sent to the client of hune-source gives the server error 42601. Through the binary it gives 422 sql_invalid. The proxy shows no message Query ('Q') whose text is not a constant of 8a.5 or 8a.12.
PS10Each row of the table of 7.25 gives its result at save and at run. Each refused function gives sql_invalid. A refused name inside a comment or a string gives no refusal.
PS11The probe. The role hune_ro gives up. Each deviant role gives misconfigured, and the log line source.misconfigured names its check (R1, R2, R3, R4, R7, R8). The advice A1 to A4 each write one source.advice line for one change.
PS12TLS. The test CA with channel binding succeeds. A wrong tls.server_name, a certificate from another CA, and a server with the method md5 or password each stop the connection. sslmode = "disable" works on the loopback interface. No system CA store is read.
PS13A stopped PostgreSQL: the panel query fails with a retryable source_unavailable, /readyz stays 200, and the state is down. After the restart, the state is up.
PS14The pool. After 70 s without a query, the next query succeeds on the first attempt. A connection that the admin session ends before BEGIN READ ONLY gives one retry and a success. A connection that it ends after BEGIN READ ONLY gives 503 source_unavailable, and the proxy shows no second run.
PS15Attribution. pg_stat_activity holds the application_name and the comment of 8a.11 with the query_id and the uid. For 1,000 random subjects, the comment never closes before its end.
PS16A custom variable on the source. An open variable with the typed value x' OR 1=1 -- in a string gives the escaped literal, and the result has no row. A closed variable refuses a value outside the options with 422 variable_value_refused, and no query reaches the source.
PS17Annotations on the source, through the server (7.37). A query of node_load with $__time, $__timeFilter, a region of 30 s, a text[] of tags and the variable env gives the marks in the order of the time. The log line has the kind annotation, and pg_stat_activity is not read. The same times come from an int8 of epoch milliseconds. 1,000 rows give 1,000 marks, and 1,001 rows give 422 query_limit. An empty result gives no mark. A text with an HTML tag gives 422 annotation_invalid with no cell value. A seconds column of $__timeEpoch gives marks in January 1970 (7.37, the table of the kinds).

16.9b MongoDB source integration cases

The mongosource job runs these cases on MongoDB 7.0 and on the newest 8.x release. Each version has a tag and a digest in tests/images.lock. Each test server runs in a disposable container on 127.0.0.1, with synthetic data only. The server runs with authorization on, TLS only and --noscripting.

A second server runs without --noscripting for the case MS18. A third server is a replica set of two members in one container for the case MS12 and the case MS09. The key hosts needs 2 to 7 members. Each port is a free port of 127.0.0.1, taken at the start of the job (16.17a).

At its first start, each server runs tests/fixtures/mongo-source/init.js. This file holds these items:

  • the database analytics. Its collections are node_load, node_events, types, big and secret. node_load holds 1,000,000 documents made by a loop. types has one field of each BSON type. big holds one document of 10 MiB. The key collections does not list secret;
  • a second database other with one collection;
  • the user hune_ro of 8b.13, with the role read on analytics;
  • deviant users, one for each check:
    • a user with readWrite, one with readAnyDatabase and one with clusterMonitor (M2);
    • one with a custom role that holds insert, and one with a custom role on anyResource (M3);
    • one that lives in a database other than the key auth_database (M1). Its login fails first, so the probe never reaches the check M1;
  • the user hune_rw, which has readWrite on analytics, for MS09;
  • the users hune_sha1 and hune_plain, which have the contract of hune_ro. Their mechanisms are SCRAM-SHA-1 and PLAIN. MS10 uses them.

The file tests/fixtures/mongo-source/apply.sh runs init.js with mongosh. It gives the passwords to mongosh as variables, so init.js holds no password.

The cases use three clients. The first is the binary of the build. The second is the client of the crate hune-source, called directly. It runs no grammar check, because the check is in hune-core, so it sends any pipeline that the wire guard accepts. The third is a plain driver client of the test, with no option of 8b.4, and mongosh.

A proxy on 127.0.0.1 sits between a client and the server in the cases that name it. It ends the TLS on both sides with the test certificate, and it records the name of each command. It can close the connections, delay the answers, and close a connection after a given command. In MS09 the test uses the client of the crate hune-source with the user hune_rw, because the probe refuses this user.

The name of each test starts with its case id in lower case, for example ms04_row_limit_is_exact. The script tests/ci/mongosource.sh reports each case MS01 to MS18 as PASS, FAIL or SKIP, and it prints a summary for each version. It gives the connection to the tests in variables with the prefix HUNE_TEST_MGS_. The program of Hune reads none of them. A run with no network sets HUNE_MGS_NO_PULL=1, and the script then uses the local images mongo:7.0 and mongo:8.0.

The script tests/fixtures/mongo-source/demo.sh starts a disposable demo server, makes a demo database from synthetic data, and prints a mongodb "mgdemo" block for the configuration. The options --dev and --down of tests/ci/mongosource.sh start the servers of one version with no test, and remove them.

The cases of the first part need no driver and no server. They run with the fake client of 8b.16: MS02 (the grammar part), MS03 (the expansion part), MS06 (the golden frames), MS11 (the check functions) and MS07 (the refusal part). They are in the job rust, and a build with no mongo-driver feature runs them.

Each of these five cases has a second part that runs on a real server. The cases that need a real server are in the job mongosource. The tests of MS13, MS16 and MS17 start a server of the crate hune in the process of the test, with the real driver.

CaseCheck
MS01Each macro of 7.34, expanded and run on a seed collection with known times, gives the expected documents. $__timeFrom and $__timeTo give Date values with the millisecond of from_ms and to_ms. $__interval_s is the binSize of $dateTrunc for each step of the interval table of 7.4.
MS02Each stage and each operator of 7.31 runs on the seed data and gives the frame of its golden file in tests/golden/pipeline-mongo/. The golden files are Extended JSON. The part with no server checks that each name of 7.31 passes the grammar and that each other name fails it.
MS03For 2,000 random values of each run (16.4), a query that holds the value in a whole-leaf placeholder and in an embedded placeholder returns the value, unchanged, as a document. The values hold $, $$, ., quote marks, backslashes, {, }, U+2028 and long texts. A value that starts with $ is refused (7.35, PM2). No query reads a field that the value names.
MS04Limits. A result of exactly max_result_rows documents passes, and one more gives 422 query_limit. A result above max_result_bytes gives its code. A pipeline above max_execution_time gives 504 query_timeout. A command above max_query_size gives 422 sql_too_large. A $group over node_load with a large $push and allowDiskUse: false gives 422 query_memory_limit. A document of 10 MiB with max_result_bytes of 1 MiB gives 422 query_limit, and the client reads no second document. A cut result is never shown as complete.
MS05Cancellation. The test starts a query that runs 30 s and has a cursor, then closes its client connection. currentOp shows no operation with the comment of the query within 2 s. The same holds at the client deadline. A query that has no cursor yet leaves currentOp within maxTimeMS plus 2 s. The proxy shows one killCursors.
MS06Each type of 7.16c gives its kind and its value: the Int64 maximum, a Decimal128 with a scale, NaN and the infinities, a Date with milliseconds and before 1970, an ObjectId, a UUID, a nested document, an array, a missing field. A column that mixes Int32 and Double passes, and a column that mixes Int32 and String gives 422 query_error with mongo_code null. A column of each unsupported type gives the same error. A result with no document gives a frame with no column. A document with 257 fields gives the error of the column limit.
MS07Each row of 7.32 and each name that no table of 7.31 lists gives 422 sql_invalid at run and 422 invalid_document at save. The proxy shows no aggregate or find for any of them. A refused name inside a string value gives no refusal. A key that starts with $ and is not an operator gives the refusal.
MS08The user side alone. mongosh logs in as hune_ro. Each statement of 8b.13 fails with the code that 8b.13 gives. A read of the collection secret works, because the role reads it, and Hune refuses it by the list (7.33).
MS09The connection side alone. The client of hune-source connects with the user hune_rw. A request with $out, with $merge, with $function and with $where at the end of a pipeline, at the first level and in a $facet, is refused by the wire guard, and the proxy shows no command for it. The proxy shows only the commands of 8b.5 for a run of all the cases of this table. With read_preference = "secondary" on the replica set, a pipeline with $out or $merge that the test sends with a plain client as hune_ro fails with the code 13. A secondary forwards the write of the user hune_rw, so this user is not in the test. No call of a write function exists in the code (16.18).
MS10The link. The test CA succeeds. A certificate with another host name, a certificate from another CA, and a server that offers only SCRAM-SHA-1 or PLAIN each stop the connection. tls_mode = "disable" works on the loopback interface. The tests show that no SRV or TXT lookup and no system CA store exist, because the lock file shows no crate for them in the closure of the driver. A test with a TLS server that has a certificate of another CA fails, and the right CA passes the handshake. A CA file that changed after the start is refused. The environment variables of 13.24 change nothing: the test runs again with them set.
MS11The probe. The user hune_ro gives up. Each deviant user gives misconfigured, and the log line source.misconfigured names its check (M1, M2 or M3). The advice A1 and A2 each write one source.advice line for one change. A version below 7.0 writes source.version_unsupported. The part with no server runs the check functions on the structures of 8b.16.
MS12The replica set. A block with the topology replica_set and the list of the two members gives up. A list that lacks a member, or a set name that differs, gives misconfigured with the check M4. The driver gets no set name, so the reason is M4 and not a timeout.
MS13A stopped MongoDB: the panel query fails with a retryable source_unavailable, /readyz stays 200, and the state is down. After the restart, the state is up.
MS14The pool. After 70 s without a query, the next query succeeds on the first attempt. A connection that the admin session closes before the command goes out gives one retry and a success. A connection that the proxy closes after the command went out gives 503 source_unavailable, and the proxy shows no second command.
MS15Attribution. currentOp and the profiler hold the comment of 8b.11 with the query_id and the uid. For 1,000 random subjects, the comment fits 1,024 bytes.
MS16An options query (7.14) and a preview run on a MongoDB source. With server.query_inspect = true the inspect text is the compact Extended JSON of the expanded tree and holds no member that Hune adds.
MS17The golden pipelines of tests/golden/pipeline-mongo/ give the same result at save and at run, on the same tree. The saved SHA-256 of the expanded command is the value in the log line query.run.
MS18The server without --noscripting. A plain client runs $where and the test shows that the server permits it. Hune refuses the same text by the grammar, and the proxy shows no command. This case records the known limit of 8b.15.
MS19A custom variable on the source. An open variable with a typed value gives the value as a leaf, and a value that starts with $ gives 422 variable_value_refused. A closed variable refuses a value outside the options, and no command reaches the source.
MS20Annotations on the source, through the server with the real driver (7.37). A pipeline on node_events with $__timeFrom, $__timeTo, a $match on level and a $project of time, timeEnd ($add of 30 s), title, text and the array tags gives the marks in the order of the time. The same times come from an Int64 of epoch milliseconds ($toLong). 1,000 rows give 1,000 marks, and 1,001 rows give 422 query_limit. An empty result has no column and gives no mark. A text with an HTML tag gives 422 annotation_invalid with no cell value.

The test file crates/hune-source/tests/mongosource_hello.rs makes sure that the hello of the driver holds no value of the environment (8b.15). It is not a case MS.

A test cannot set a variable in its own process, so a parent test starts the test binary again with the variables set. It sets AWS_EXECUTION_ENV, AWS_REGION, K_SERVICE, VERCEL, FUNCTIONS_WORKER_RUNTIME and KUBERNETES_SERVICE_HOST, together and in groups. A stub server records the hello, or the proxy of this section does so with a real server. The test fails when the key client.env is present, or when the name or the value of a variable is in the message. The tests with the stub need no server. The test with the proxy runs in the job mongosource.

16.10 Browser tests

The tests use Playwright in the test image tests/browser/Dockerfile. npm runs only inside this image. The image uses a committed package-lock.json. The tests run against the release build of the binary in compose, with ClickHouse and seed data. All cases run in Chromium. The cases B01, B02, B03, B07, B08, B15, B16, B18, B103, B112, B122, B141, B163 and B172 also run in Firefox.

The runner drives Firefox with WebDriver BiDi: set HUNE_BROWSER=firefox (tests/browser/lib/bidi.mjs). Chrome is the default and its client is not changed.

WebKit has no driver on the test host. The library libwebkit2gtk-4.1 and its MiniBrowser are present, but no WebKitWebDriver. The runner cannot drive WebKit, so all WebKit checks are manual (16.19).

The cases B170 and B171 depend on the engine of the text area, and Firefox cannot run them as they are. WebDriver BiDi presses the modifier keys as real keys, so the page also sees the keydown of Control and Shift. It cannot send a key with a code that its character does not give (the layout check of B170). It has no IME command (B171). These two cases are a manual check in Firefox and WebKit before 1.0.0 (16.19).

Firefox has no media emulation in WebDriver BiDi. On Firefox the transport sets prefers-color-scheme through the theme control of 9.25 (the key hune.theme and the event hune-theme). The cases test both themes, but not the media query. A case that needs the print media or prefers-reduced-motion is not supported on Firefox.

The case B153 has its own limit of 900 s. Its states and its hover probe add about 170 probes to the 317 control changes that it made before F43.

B01The list page shows one card for each dashboard, and no folder title for one folder. A card is an article with a title link and the tags. A dashboard of a volume has no badge of the last change. The history keeps edit=1, and its "Back to the dashboard" shows the state edit with the draft. edit-off with changes shows the dialog, and each of its three buttons does what 10.2 says. After the exit, the page has no hook of the editor (edit-grid, edit-bar). The stack after each open and leave pair is the same as before.
B76Back and Forward (10.2a, rules 9 and 6). A scripted walk goes through the seven states by their controls. Then the case presses Back to the start and Forward to the end. After each press, data-hune-view and the URL are the state of the entry, the page shows one view, and the page writes replaceState only for the normal form. With changes in the draft, a Back out of edit mode keeps the draft and shows the notice with "Resume" and "Discard". "Resume" opens the state edit with the draft. An open Save drawer closes at each press.
B77Reload (10.2a, rule 10). For each of the seven states, the case reloads the page. The state, the URL and data-hune-origin are the same. A sampler of frames (requestAnimationFrame, from the first frame) sees no frame with the hold off and a view that is not the state, no frame with an empty main area, and no change of more than 0.5 px of the toolbar. leave after the reload uses history.back(). A draft comes back with the notice "Your draft is back.".
B78Deep links (10.2a, rules 1, 3, 4 and 11). The case loads the vectors of FE-26: each URL with two or more view names, with an unknown id, with an unknown value and with the form panel-<id>. For each, data-hune-view is the state of the vector, the URL is the normal form, the page shows one view, and a notice shows for an id that does not exist. The URL with editPanel and viewPanel shows the panel editor and no panel view.
B79Rights (10.2a, rule 8, and 10.14). On the server with no primary store: each deep link with edit names shows the state view or panel-view, the notice "You cannot edit this dashboard.", the URL with no edit name, and no "Resume" notice, also when a draft is in localStorage. The draft stays. editview=history shows the history with no "Restore". On the server with a primary store, the page injects editing = false in the session and presses Back and Forward over edit entries: the same result. A save that gets 403 keeps edit mode and the draft.
B80Placed controls (4.7a, 9.10). A dashboard places env (select), node (multi, All, hide = "label") and min_weight (number, title "Threshold", second section). account has no placement and region has hide = "variable". Each hook variable-<name> occurs one time and is inside its frame. The frames have the grid places of the placements, and the bar shows account only. A change of env writes var-env and runs the stat with the new value. A change of node and an entry of min_weight that breaks the number grammar behave as in the bar (B08). A reload with var-env in the URL shows the value in the frame.
B81The row under the header has no height when no variable is left (9.10). A dashboard whose variables are all placed (or have hide = "variable") and that has no dashboard link shows no row: its height is 0, and the first panel starts at the same place at the first frame and after the load of the options (the probe of B58, no move of more than 0.5 px). With a dashboard link, the row stays and holds the link only. A dashboard with variables that have no placement shows the bar with exactly these variables.
B82Keyboard and accessibility of a placed control (9.26). The Tab order is the order of the grid. Enter, Space, the arrows, Escape and the search field work in a frame as in the bar. After Escape, the focus returns to the button. A text control applies on Enter and on blur. The accessible names are <label>: <text>, also with hide = "label". The frame has role="group" and the name of its title, else of its label. The draft in the local storage has transparent = true. The test then saves the document. In the saved view, the 10 changed panels have no fill and no edge at rest, and the edge shows on hover. The panel that the test did not change keeps its fill and its edge. Three panels that are transparent show the states loading, error and empty. Their frame has no fill, and the text of each state has a contrast ratio of 4.5 or more against the page colour. The case fails when the switch has no effect.
B153The audit of the options of the panel editor (10.3, 10.3a, 10.3h). For each type, the test builds the states of its table (10.3h, rule 14), lists the controls of each state, and changes each one like a person does. It reads the draft in the local storage and a print of the preview (the markup of the frame, a hash of each canvas, and the card of the tooltip after a hover). The rules are F1 to F6: the draft changes in the member of the control and in the writes of its row only; the preview changes, unless the control is in the closed list of 30 entries at most; the preview comes back to its first print after the old value is put back; a stale entry and a dead entry fail; each row of each table has a control, and each control has a row and a data-option; no control is skipped. It also adds an item for each button "Add" on a fresh page, sets the repeat variable, the direction and the maximum for each row, and saves a title, a description and a width to read them in the view. The case has its own time limit of 900 s.
B160A closed query variable (9.10). A URL value that is not an option gives the default, the first option or "All", with one notice (data-notice="variable") and the new value in the URL. A multi URL with one wrong value keeps the others. A change of the range with refresh = time_change that removes the selected option gives the same result. An options error and an empty list give no notice. A reload of the new URL gives no notice.
B161An open query variable that is not multi. A typed value shows the row Use, Enter selects it, the URL holds it, and a reload keeps it. A change of the range keeps it. The reopened popup shows the typed row as the first row, selected. No request goes out while the person types (the count of requests). A text that equals an option gives no row Use.

Enter in an empty field applies, the URL repeats var-<name> in the order, and the select shows the texts. "All" and a typed value exclude each other.

B171The folded view and the edit (10.3e). B218The ad hoc filters (9.10a, 4.10c). A server of the case has adhoc_columns. The form of the bar offers the permitted keys of 6.14 and the operators = and !=. A filter added with the form runs panel 1 (the macro) again and not panel 2, with the closed request member. The address holds the item of Grafana, two filters give two items in order, and a remove runs panel 1 again and gives the focus to the plus button. A link of Grafana 12.4.2 with %21%3D, a label after , and __gfp__ opens with its filters. A hostile value matches no row.
B219The refusals of the page for the ad hoc filters (9.10a). An address with a key that is not permitted, the operator >, a control character, two parts and 17 valid items keeps 16 filters and shows the notice with the dropped items. The address then holds 16 items, and the plus button is disabled. The form refuses a value of more than 1,024 bytes, with the error, and sends nothing. A server whose list holds one key gives that key alone in 6.14, and an item of another key is dropped.
B225The gauge (4.33e, 9.19g). A dashboard has eight gauges: three sizes, threshold labels, a percent field with a neutral value, a grid of five, a row of three, a column of six, a query with no row, and a fixed colour with a hostile field name. In both themes, the record data-gauge of each gauge equals the rules of 9.19g for the box of the page and the widths of its font, within 0.01 px. The pixels show the filled arc, the empty arc, the band of the thresholds and no paint inside the arc. The canvases hold the value texts, the labels and "No data". The column of six keeps 75 px and scrolls. A title and the description are text nodes, and the panel has no image element. The colours of the light theme are the light shades.
B180A table with organize (rename, hide, order), then sort_by and limit. The header shows the new names, the order and the cut. "View data" shows the same.
B181A stat and a table with calculate_field in the four modes. The new field shows with its default name, its alias, and with replace_fields.
B182A table with two queries and merge. One frame shows, with no select of frames. A merge of a panel with one query does nothing.
B183A time series with two queries and join_by_field (null key, outer). One chart draws, with the legend A a and B b. The mode inner removes the rows of one query.
B184A time series with a long table and partition_by_values. One series draws for each value. A table shows each frame in its select with the option text of 9.22a.
B185A join_by_field of three frames with 99,000 rows each shows the error of the cap (rule 13). The query limit of 100,000 rows bounds each frame, so two frames cannot go above the cap. A partition_by_values of 150 values shows the error of the frame cap. Both leave the other panels as they were.
B186The editor adds each of the eleven kinds, edits its fields, moves it, removes it, and shows an error of 4.37. The JSON tab and the draft agree.
B187The import of a Grafana dashboard with the eleven ids shows the report of 15.6 and the panels with the new kinds.
B188A reuse panel with with_transforms applies the new kinds of its source and of its own, in this order.
B189The names <img src=x onerror=alert(1)> and a name with $ as a rename, an alias and a converted value show as text. No script runs, and the CSP report is empty.
B220The logs panel (4.33f, 9.19h). In both themes: the time in the zone of the dashboard with milliseconds, the level texts and the colours of Grafana 12.4.2, lines of 28 px with a text of 14 px on 22 px, 12 px between the columns, a level column of the width of the longest level text, newest first and oldest first, wrap (several rows, no side scroll) and no wrap (one row and a side scroll), and the plain items with details = false. The details row opens under the line with a click and closes with a second click; the arrow keys, Home and End move the one tab stop, and Enter opens the details row.
B221The logs panel and its threat T104 (4.33f, 7.16e, 9.19h). Hostile bodies, levels and field values stay text: no element and no event attribute come from the text, and no script runs. 10,001 rows give 10,000 lines and the note of the cap of lines. 201 bodies of 20,000 characters give 199 lines of 10,000 characters and the sign of the cut. A query with no row shows "No data". A frame with no text column shows the message. The save of a table with the format logs gives 422 with not_permitted at the pointer of the format.
B226The map with no tile source (9.19i, 12.8). The header of each kind of answer is the header of 12.8, byte for byte, and the session member geomap is null. A map of four points and one bad row draws the plain background and four markers, with the note of the skipped row. The pixel of a marker has the fill of dark-green at the alpha 0.16. The view fit frames the points. The card of a marker shows a hostile name as text, with no element and no script. The buttons and the keys change the zoom, and a button is off at zoom 0. No request of the page leaves the origin of the page.
B227The map with a tile source on 127.0.0.1 (9.19i, 12.8, 13.12c). The case starts a server with a block geomap, a tile server of its own with TLS on 127.0.0.1, and a Chrome of its own that maps the test name tiles.hune.test to 127.0.0.1 and gets no other host. The header has the tile source in img-src only, and the session gives the three values. Each tile request has a path of three whole numbers, no Referer, no cookie (a cookie of the test host exists) and no authorization. The page has 6 requests in flight at most. A pan makes no request, and the first request comes 200 ms after the release. A host with no CORS header gives no tile and no second request in the view. A tile of 512 px is dropped. The attribution is text. With refresh=10s and no CORS header, the host gets one request for each tile of the view in 25 s, through three runs of the panel. A pan survives a refresh, and the new draw asks for no tile. A map of 24 tiles of 256 px with 16 MiB of padding each grows the renderers by less than 64 MiB (security review 3, findings F1 and F5).
B228The cards of the list page (9.7). A server with the default threshold and three dashboards in two folders shows no sort control and no tag filter. The page has no title and no counter, and no word about the access. Two folders give two folder titles in capitals, with a menu of the folder. A card is 400 px wide. The title is on one line with an ellipsis and the full name in its title attribute. The block of cards is centred, across and down, in a window that is taller than the block. In a short window it starts at the top and the page scrolls. At 390 px there is one column. A card that Hune never saved has no badge of the last change. A card that Hune saved has the badge Last change <age> at the top right, and the cards keep one height. A writable card has the three dots at the top right, after the badge, and no other button. The menu has "Edit", "Export", "Move" and "Delete" in this order. "Edit" links to ?edit=1 and opens the edit mode. "Delete" opens the delete dialog with the name, and nothing is deleted. A threshold equal to the count hides the filters, and a lower threshold shows them. With the filters hidden, q has no effect. A server with editing off shows a card with no three dots. The sort control and the tag filter open a menu that touches the button, they open with the key Down, they mark the chosen line with a check, and Escape gives the focus back to the button. The tag menu has a chip and a count on each line.
B229The marks of the edit mode on the panel frames (10.16). The view has no resize handle (.panel__resize), no data-drag-handle and no aria-keyshortcuts. In edit mode each panel has one handle that shows, one drag mark on its title bar and one set of key shortcuts. After the switch "Edit" goes off, after "Discard" in the exit dialog, and after "Revert all changes" followed by the switch, none of them stays on the frames that the view grid uses again. After "Revert all changes" the edit mode goes on with one handle for each panel, and no handle is made twice. A new entry gives them again.
B230The id of a new folder (10.7, 10.12). The dialog "New folder" has the field "Title" only. The page makes the id from the title: "Équipe réseau" gives equipe-reseau. The same title again gives an id with a dash and four characters, and the two folders have the same title. A title of symbols gives folder- and four characters. The dialog "Import" has the last entry "New folder..." in the select of the folders. The entry shows a field for the title. B231The range and the refresh in edit mode (9.5, 10.2a, rule 12). A person chooses "Last 24 hours" and the refresh "30s" in edit mode. After the switch goes off, the controls and the address still hold them. The same holds for the entry by ?edit=1 and for a save that has no default box. The saved defaults do not change.
B232The defaults from the Save dialog (10.9). With no difference, "Save" is not active. A different range makes "Save" active and "Revert" not. The dialog shows one box for each value that differs, both off at first, with the hint of the current value and the default. The range alone, then the refresh alone, change only their own members of the saved document. The address then names only the values that still differ.
B233The view of one panel has no page scrollbar (9.8). At 1440x900, 1280x720, 768x600 and 390x844, in the light and the dark theme, for a dashboard without variables and a dashboard with variables: the document and the body are not taller than the window, and no vertical scrollbar takes room from the page. With variables the panel ends at least one page padding above the window edge.
B240The JSON view of the edit mode (10.4). The text has colours: a key, a text, a number and the punctuation each have a colour, in the light and the dark theme, with 4.5:1 or more on the area. The bars of the gutter have 3:1 or more. The text area is transparent, and the first row of the layer is the first line of the text. A draft with no change has no mark. A changed line gets modified, two new lines get added, a deleted line leaves a mark at the border, and a syntax fault marks the line of the list of errors. A revert gives back the saved text and clears the marks. The area ends one page padding above the edge of the window, and the page has no scroll, at 1440x900, 1280x720, 768x900 and 390x800, with and without the list of errors. A text of 6,000 lines draws a window of rows and not all of them, a jump draws the row at the top, and a text of one line has 2,000 coloured parts at most.

Each card of the store has the lock and no three dots, and all cards have one height. The menu "New" lists New dashboard, New folder and Import.
